{{Short description|None}}

[[Masters athletics]] is a class of the sport of [[athletics (sport)|athletics]] for athletes of over 35 years of age. The events include [[track and field]], [[road running]] and [[cross country running]]. These are the current world records in various five-year-groups, maintained by [[World Masters Athletics|WMA]], the World Association of Masters Athletes, which is designated by the [[IAAF|World Athletics]] (formerly IAAF) to conduct the worldwide sport of Masters (Veterans) Athletics (Track and Field).<ref>{{cite web|title=The WMA Organizational Goals|url=http://www.world-masters-athletics.org/aboutus.htm|publisher=[[World Masters Athletics|WMA]]|access-date=26 November 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161127084730/http://www.world-masters-athletics.org/aboutus.htm|archive-date=27 November 2016|url-status=live}}</ref> Starting at age 35, each age group starts on the athlete's birthday in years that are evenly divisible by 5 and extends until the next such occurrence.  For record purposes, older athletes are not included in younger age groups, except in the case of relay team members.  A relay team's age group is determined by the age of the youngest member.

Some Masters events (hurdles, throwing implements) have modified specifications.  The [[Track and field#Combined events|combined events]] use an age-graded result applied against the standard scoring table.

===Javelin throw Women===
Effective the 2014 season, [[World Masters Athletics|WMA]] increased the weight of the javelin for women 60–75.  Until the records of the lighter implements are surpassed, two records are officially kept.<ref name=wjav>{{cite web | url=http://masterstrack.com/wma-throws-out-javelin-specs-records-for-w60-w65-and-w70/ | title=WMA throws out javelin specs, records for W60, W65 and W70 | date=11 January 2014 }}</ref>
